# Decks: documented functions (`deck`)

Reference table generated in the documentation round of 2026-10-31 and extended in the rounds of 2026-11-03 and 2026-11-04. Each row is a function that reached the `documented` stage (`docs/PROGRESS.md`): its prototype, purpose, return value, side effects and callers, read from the Ghidra decompilation with the names of that round. The same text is the function's plate comment in Ghidra (`re/documented*.tsv`, applied by `tools/ghidra/ApplySignatures.java`). How the subsystem works as a whole is in [`../deck.md`](../deck.md); pointers to structures are typed `void *` and the structure is named in the summary.

Functions: 644 (BOOT.BIN 50, rel_charalist 1, rel_conv_machine 1, rel_deck 88, rel_deckswap 132, rel_decktutorial 165, rel_duelrecord 1, rel_limitlist 4, rel_password 1, rel_recipeviewer 58, rel_select_card 142, rel_tutoriallist 1).

Returns 0 when invalid, 2 when valid with card-list entries needed, 1 when valid otherwise. No side effects. No direct caller; exported, module callers via import stubs: rel_title(ui_title_check_deck_valid_copy); 7 further import stubs without callers. |

Side effects: deck_get_duel_setup_buffer() + 0xc0. No direct caller. Exported; called from rel_title (2: ui_title_start_wireless_duel, ui_title_start_comm_duel). |

## `rel_deck`

| Address | Prototype | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x08e9aa00` | `void deck_alloc_editor_list_buffers(void *list, int kind)` | Allocates the arrays of an editor list from heap *0x08ebf188 using the capacity u16 at 0x08ebd234 + 2 x kind: an entry array of capacity x 6 bytes at list +4 and a view array of capacity x 2 bytes at list +0x10 (nothing when the capacity is 0). Side effects: heap allocations, list +4, +0x10. Callers: 1 (deck_init_editor_lists). |
| `0x08e9aa90` | `void deck_init_editor_lists(void)` | Builds the deck editor lists: for another owner's deck loads the slot's recipe into 0x08ebee20; for each list kind with a builder in the table at 0x08ebd21c allocates its arrays, stores the kind, fills it with the builder, updates totals and the view and sorts it; then makes list 4 an alias of list 2, resets the trunk view, counts frames and flags the owner's recipe cards. Side effects: editor lists 0x08ebed00-0x08ebedbf, heap. Callers: 1 (deck_editor_init_state). |
| `0x08e9abc4` | `int deck_build_trunk_list(void *list)` | Trunk builder: for ids 0xe88-0x1f39 computes the free copies (own deck: deck_count_free_card_copies_2; another owner's: deck_get_other_deck_free_copies(slot, id, 0x08ebee20)) and adds an entry with that many copies for valid cards with free copies > 0; counts the ids with owned copies (or allowance) > 0 in 0x08ebee0e. Returns the entry count. Side effects: list arrays, 0x08ebee0e. No direct caller (builder table 0x08ebd21c). |
| `0x08e9ad8c` | `int deck_build_main_deck_list(void *list)` | Main-deck list builder: for each card of list 1 of the edited slot (deck_get_slot_count/deck_get_slot_card) writes an entry {id, image index (ui_find_card_image_index, resource 0x8031), 1 copy, flags cleared} and its view index; for the player's own deck each save card-list entry with state 1 sets byte 4 bit 7 on the last matching entry that lacks it; entries whose id is in the slot's key-card list (deck_slot_key_list_contains) get the key flag (byte 5 bit 5). Returns the entry count. Side effects: the list's entry and view arrays. Callers: 2 (deck_reload_editor_lists, deck_init_editor_lists). |
| `0x08e9aff0` | `int deck_build_side_deck_list(void *list)` | Side-deck list builder: for each card of list 2 of the edited slot writes an entry {id, image index (resource 0x8031), 1 copy, flags cleared} and its view index; for the player's own deck each save card-list entry with state 2 sets byte 4 bit 7 on the last matching entry that lacks it; entries whose id is in the slot's key-card list get the key flag (byte 5 bit 5). Returns the entry count. Side effects: the list's entry and view arrays. No direct caller (reached through a function pointer; presumably the builder table 0x08ebd21c like the other list builders, hypothesis medium). |
| `0x08e9b254` | `int deck_build_extra_deck_list(void *list)` | Extra-deck builder: one entry (copies 1, flags cleared, image index) per card of list 3 of the edited slot; for the player's own deck, each save card-list entry with state 3 sets flag 0x80 of byte 4 on the last matching entry that lacks it. Returns the entry count. Side effects: list arrays. No direct caller (builder table 0x08ebd21c). |
| `0x08e9b448` | `int deck_build_card_list_pool(void *list)` | List builder: unless another owner's deck is edited, adds one entry per save card-list entry whose state is not 1, 2 or 3 (id, image index from ui_find_card_image_index with resource 0x8031, copies 1 with flag 0x80, byte 5 bits 0, 5, 6 cleared) and its view index; returns the entry count. Side effects: list arrays. No direct caller (builder table 0x08ebd21c). |
| `0x08e9b5c0` | `void deck_copy_editor_list(void *dst, void *src, int realloc_view)` | Makes dst an alias of editor list src: when realloc_view is set, frees dst's view array (+0x10) and allocates a new 0x28-byte one from the editor heap *0x08ebf188; copies the entry-array pointer (+4, shared, not duplicated), entry count (+8) and view count (+0x14), and memcpy's view-count x 2 bytes of view indices. Side effects: dst, heap. Callers: 2 (deck_init_editor_lists, deck_reload_editor_lists). |
| `0x08e9b648` | `void deck_free_editor_lists(void)` | For the six editor lists at 0x08ebed00 (0x20 bytes each) frees the view array (+0x10) and, except for list 4 whose entries are shared with list 2, the entry array (+4) on heap *0x08ebf188, clearing the pointers. Side effects: heap frees, list pointers. Callers: 1 (deck_editor_scene_exit). |

| `0x00000000` | `int deck_swap_module_start(void)` | Module entry point of rel_deckswap (ELF e_entry 0, the first 8 bytes of the module), with nothing to do. Returns 0 at once. No side effects. No direct caller (run by the module loader through e_entry). |
| `0x00000008` | `void deck_swap_start(uint *deck, int card_list_count, ushort *card_list_states, int own_heap)` | Sub-module start of the deck swap screen. Clears the work blocks 0x00018cd0 (0x304 bytes) and 0x00018fe0 (0x6c4 bytes). With own_heap == 1 creates an FPL of 0x44e000 bytes, makes a heap 0x00018fb8 in it, allocates 0x9f000 bytes and opens a cardh.cip reader there (card_db_open_cardh_cip, 0x00018fbc); otherwise allocates 0x3af000 bytes from the duel-draw heap (system_duel_draw_get_heap) for the heap and reuses the large card-art reader (ui_get_large_card_art_reader). Stores the 0xc0-byte deck struct pointer (0x00018fa8), the save card-list count (0x00018fac) and the per-item card-list state array (0x00018fb0), lowers the thread attribute, starts the resource load (0x000099b8), sets font options by language (save_get_language 0 = Japanese) and sets the state word 0x00018cd4 to 0. Returns nothing. Side effects: those globals, heap/FPL allocation, font flags. Callers: exported; module callers via import stubs: rel_labo (1: FUN_00000034). |
| `0x000001dc` | `bool deck_swap_update(void)` | Sub-module update. State 0 (0x00018cd4) waits for the card reader (card_db_cip_is_ready) and the resource load (0x00009a38), then hides the loading overlay, fades in over 16 frames, goes to state 1, arms the 0x1518-frame (90 s) timer 0x00018fb4 when a network session is active, and starts music track 9 when duel set-up byte +0x194 is 4 or 5, else track 8. State 9 waits for the fade, then goes to 10; state 10 shows the loading overlay and returns the result of deck_swap_release_resources. Other states run the per-frame tick (0x000095f0) and deck_swap_handle_input. Returns true when the screen has finished and released its resources. Side effects: state word, fades, music, timer. Callers: exported; module callers via import stubs: rel_labo (1: FUN_00000034). |
| `0x0000031c` | `int deck_swap_release_resources(void)` | Teardown state machine on 0x00018cda. Step 0 frees the resources (0x00009adc) and the list buffers (deck_swap_free_lists); with an own FPL (0x00018fc8 > 0) it asks the cardh reader to stop and moves to step 1, else frees the heap (system_free_aligned) and its duel-draw block and returns 1. Step 1 waits for card_db_cip_is_idle and moves to 2; step 2 frees the reader buffer, the FPL block and deletes the FPL, then returns 1. Returns 1 when done, 0 while waiting (1 for any other step value). Side effects: 0x00018cda, heap and FPL frees. Callers: 1 (deck_swap_update). |

## `rel_decktutorial`

| Address | Prototype | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x00000000` | `void deck_tutorial_draw(void)` | Draw callback of the deck tutorial (export 3 of libdeck_tutorial_rel). Clears layer 0 to black; unless the scene state 0x22c32 is 0 or 4 it draws the editor screen (0x17574) and then, on layer 4, the tutorial overlays: when bit 0 of 0x22ff4 is set only the quit dialog window 0x22ff8; otherwise the highlight (deck_tutorial_draw_highlight with region 0x22c80, flag 0x2 of 0x22c3e), the allowed-button icons (flag 0x4), the demo button icons while the wait state 0x22c3d is 9, the shoulder hint, and, only while bit 0 of 0x22c3c is set (L not held), the message window (0x1af6c, bit 0 of 0x22c92), the label window 0x22c94 (flag 0x1; Japanese uses a font end-y offset of 0xc0), the panel (deck_tutorial_draw_panel, bit 1 of 0x22c92) and the picture window 0x22ed4 (flag 0x40); ends with system_draw_fade. No return value. Side effects: GE display lists of layers 0 and 4. No direct caller; exported, module callers via import stubs: rel_story(world_state_update_deck_tutorial), rel_deck(ui_draw_deck_editor_frame), rel_title(ui_title_draw_deck_tutorial). |
| `0x000002a0` | `void * deck_tutorial_draw_highlight(void *list, int region)` | Draws the highlight of opcode 0x05: copies the vertex list of the region from the table 0x211fc (8-byte entries: u16 vertex count, pointer to 12-byte vertices at +4) into inline GE data; for regions 0-3 (the editor tabs) it first patches the first quad with the tab rectangle (rectangle 0x225ac, or 0x225d8 for tab 1 when the low nibble of 0x22560 is 2 or more, widened by the tab offset from 0x4034(tab)). The vertices are drawn into the stencil buffer with a pulsing alpha (frame counter, period 16), then a 480x272 sprite with ambient colour 0xc0000000 darkens everything outside the stencil; render state is restored. Returns the advanced display-list pointer. Side effects: GE commands. Callers: 1 (deck_tutorial_draw). |
| `0x000006ec` | `void * deck_tutorial_draw_button_icon(void *list, int x, int y, int icon, int lit, int show_arrow)` | Draws one button icon from the GIM resource 0x1012 (deck_tutorial_get_resource) at (x, y): icons 0-3 are 30x27 cells at v = icon * 0x1b, u 0, or u 0x1e when lit (face buttons: 0 circle, 1 cross, 2 triangle, 3 square by the callers' bit mapping); 4 and 5 are the L and R icons (37x15, v 0x50 and 0x5f, u 0x3c, or 0x61 when lit); 6 is the select icon (32x14, v 0x6f, u 0x3e, or 99 when lit, drawn 8 pixels lower). When show_arrow is non-zero an animated 26x32 arrow (3 frames at u 0x62 + frame * 0x1a, frame = (frame counter >> 3) % 3) is drawn 0x20 pixels above the icon. Returns the advanced display-list pointer (unchanged when the resource is missing). Side effects: GE commands. Callers: 3 (deck_tutorial_draw_demo_buttons, deck_tutorial_draw_shoulder_hint, deck_tutorial_draw_allowed_buttons). |
| `0x00000920` | `void * deck_tutorial_draw_dpad_icon(void *list, int x, int y, uint lit_mask, uint pointer_mask)` | Draws the directional-pad icon from GIM 0x1012 as four 19x20 segments around (x, y): bit 0 up, bit 1 down, bit 2 left, bit 3 right; a set bit in lit_mask selects the lit cell (u 0x4f instead of 0x3c), a set bit in pointer_mask adds the animated arrow above that segment. Returns the advanced display-list pointer. Side effects: GE commands. Callers: 2 (deck_tutorial_draw_demo_buttons, deck_tutorial_draw_allowed_buttons), which always pass pointer_mask 0. |

The side deck (list 2) offers the pool or trunk likewise and, only in layouts 0-1 with a main total 0x220cc of at most 64, the extra deck for frames 3/10 (full at 20 cards) or the main deck. Trunk and pool (lists 3, 5) offer the extra deck for frames 3/10 (full at 20) or else the main deck (full above 64) plus, unless editing another deck, the side deck (full at 20); they then refuse with 4 when three same-name copies are already in lists 0-2 and with 7, 6 or 5 when the card's limit 2, 1 or 0 is reached (limit 0: deck_count_forbidden_cards against save_test_progress_flag_e9_bit0). List 4 (the alias of the side list) offers the extra or main deck with the same full tests and no refusals. With no target it returns 2. Otherwise it pre-selects the last used target (0x241f4/0x241f6) when the pane matches, shows the card (bit 0 of 0x2256c), prepares the label and window position (ui_set_card_move_label, ui_place_card_move_label), allocates and opens the 0x78-byte move window on list 0x224fc, sets editor mode 0x221c0 = 1 and move state 1, and, while the script waits with kind 3 (wait state 10, 0x22c70 = 3), increments the wait counter 0x22c34. | `0x00012a7c` | `void deck_tutorial_update_text_search_menu(void *pad)` | Per-frame step of the text search (editor mode 5) on the search record 0x24b28 (+0 state, +4 focus: 0 query box, 2 OK; +0x4c main window, +0x50 history window). Unlike rel_deck there is no branch that opens text entry on the box, so the query can only come from the history or from the script (deck_tutorial_set_search_query). State 1: the history window runs; a chosen entry is copied from 0x222a0 + 0x40 x n to the query (+6) and OK gets the focus; cancel, or Up on the first entry, returns to state 0. State 2: once the main window leaves its open state, closes the windows (state 3). State 3: when the main window has closed: if cancelled, frees the menu (ui_close_text_search_menu) and returns to editor mode 0; otherwise resets the windows, releases the filter windows, clears the window pointers and goes to state 4. ## `rel_limitlist`

| Address | Prototype | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x00000008` | `void deck_limit_list_start(uchar mode, void *heap)` | Start function of the forbidden/limited list screen (liblimitlist_rel export): clears the 0x60-byte screen block 0xa6c0, stores mode & 3 in bits 0-1 of the halfword 0xa6c4, and uses heap as the work heap 0xa6ec, or when heap is 0 creates its own: sceKernelCreateFpl (name 0xa09c, partition 2, 0x500000 bytes) into 0xa714, sceKernelAllocateFpl into 0xa718 and system_heap_create on it. Then sceKernelChangeCurrentThreadAttr(0, 0x4000), ui_limitlist_init_resources, loads BGM track 0x2c8 (audio_release_all_banks first) unless sound 2 is already playing (audio_get_sound_status bit 0), enables font shadow and colour codes, and sets font flag 1 / trim-wrap-spaces for Japanese (language 0) versus other languages. No return value. Side effects: screen block, FPL and heap, audio banks, font state. No direct caller; exported, called from rel_story (world_state_start_limit_list), rel_title (ui_title_update_limit_list) and rel_labo (0x00000034). |
| `0x00000150` | `int deck_limit_list_update(void)` | Per-frame update of the limit-list screen, a switch on the state halfword 0xa6c0: 0 loading (ui_limitlist_update_loading; when done builds the lists, state 1, fade in from black over 0x10 frames, hides the loading overlay, audio_play_track_with_fade(2)); 1 waits for the fade, then 2; 2 browsing (ui_limitlist_update_browse); 3 and 5 dialogs (ui_limitlist_update_dialog); 4 card viewer (ui_limitlist_update_card_viewer); 6 waits for the exit fade, then 7; 7 teardown (ui_limitlist_shutdown_step). Returns 0 while running, and when the teardown finishes 1, or 2 when bit 4 of byte 0xa6c5 is set (the player left through the deck-warning dialog with result 2; meaning for the caller is a hypothesis, confidence medium). Side effects: those of the state handlers. No direct caller; exported, called from rel_story (world_state_update_limit_list), rel_title (ui_title_update_limit_list) and rel_labo (0x00000034). |
| `0x000003cc` | `void deck_limit_list_draw(void)` | Draw function of the limit-list screen: clears GE layer 0 to black (colour 0xff000000, stencil 0, depth 0, clear flags 0x17). In states other than 0 and 7: when the card viewer is open (bit 3 of byte 0xa6c5) draws only ui_card_viewer_draw; otherwise on layer 4 sets texture/blend state and draws the background (ui_limitlist_draw_background), the card detail view of block 0xa6f4 (ui_draw_card_detail_view_temp), the tabs, the title bar and the cards, then either the dialog (states 3 and 5, ui_limitlist_draw_dialog) or the cursor frame (shop_draw_cursor_frame with the rectangle 0xa6d2-0xa6d8, image resource 0x8006 and the frame counter); finally the fade on layer 5 (system_draw_fade). No return value. Side effects: GE display lists of layers 0, 4 and 5. No direct caller; exported, called from rel_story (world_state_update_limit_list) and rel_title (ui_title_draw_limit_list). |
| `0x00003440` | `int deck_compare_u16_ascending(ushort *a, ushort *b)` | Same code as BOOT deck_compare_u16_ascending: returns *a - *b. No side effects. No direct caller (passed to system_qsort by ui_limitlist_init_resources). |

## `rel_recipeviewer`

| Address | Prototype | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x00000000` | `int deck_recipe_viewer_module_start(void)` | Module entry point of rel_recipeviewer (ELF e_entry 0, the first 8 bytes of the module), with nothing to do. Null tests of callback pointers show this address in the decompilation only because the module is imported at image base 0; they are compares with NULL, not references to this function. Returns 0 at once. No side effects. No direct caller (run by the module loader through e_entry). |
| `0x00000008` | `void deck_recipe_viewer_start(uchar mode, int *params, uchar own_reader, int owner)` | Sub-module start of the deck-recipe viewer. Clears the 7000-byte work block 0x0000c5e0, copies the 5-word parameter block (heap 0x0000e11c, card reader 0x0000e120, word 0x0000e124, save callback 0x0000e128, load callback 0x0000e12c), stores mode (0 browse, 1 choose a stored-deck slot to save into, 2 choose a deck to load) in the low nibble of 0x0000c5e0, own_reader in bit 0 and "owner != 1" in bit 1 of 0x0000c81d and owner in 0x0000c81e; creates an FPL of 0x468000 bytes for the heap when none was given. In owner mode it loads the owner's original slot recipe into the 0xc0-byte deck buffer 0x0000c820 (deck_load_slot_recipe(owner, 0x0000c820)) and lists only the owner's first recipe (deck_get_owner_recipe(owner, 0)) when its save recipe entry bit 0 is set; otherwise it lists every recipe 1-461 with a level whose save entry bit 0 is set (8-byte records 0x0000c902: recipe, number, entry bits 1-8, owned and total counts from deck_recipe_viewer_count_owned_copies) and the 50 stored decks (records 0x0000d772). Then starts the resource load (0x0000557c), loads music bank 0x2c8 when sound 2 is not playing, sets font options by language and starts the state machine (mode 1 skips the import scan; otherwise save_import_begin). Returns nothing. Side effects: work block, heap/FPL, audio, save import. Callers: exported; module callers via import stubs: rel_story (1: world_state_start_recipe_viewer), rel_deck (1: deck_run_recipe_viewer), rel_title (1: ui_title_update_recipe_viewer). |
| `0x00000484` | `uint deck_recipe_viewer_update(void)` | Sub-module update. Does nothing while the OSK runs (0x0000c5e4 bit 1); runs the card viewer while 0x0000c5e5 bit 2 is set; otherwise steps the reveal counter and either updates the open dialog (deck_recipe_viewer_update_dialog; a non-zero result fades out to state 6) or dispatches on the state (bits 4-11 of 0x0000c5e0): 0 import scan and resource wait, 1 tab/title frames, first deck view, fade in and music track 2, 2 wait for fade, 3 list input, 4 card input, 5 save to slot, 6 wait for fade, 7 release resources. In state 7, once released, returns -2 when cancelled, the selected index for mode 1, index \| tab << 16 for mode 2, -2 for mode 0. Returns -1 while running. Side effects: state machine, fades, music, saves through callbacks. Callers: exported; module callers via import stubs: rel_story (1: world_state_update_recipe_viewer), rel_deck (1: deck_run_recipe_viewer), rel_title (1: ui_title_update_recipe_viewer). |
| `0x000007c8` | `int deck_recipe_viewer_release_resources(void)` | Teardown steps on 0x0000c5e2: 0 with an own card reader (0x0000c81d bit 0) closes the card view and asks the reader to stop (step 1), else goes to step 2; 1 waits for card_db_cip_is_idle; 2 frees the resources (0x000058fc, own reader only), the import buffer 0x0000d904 and the FPL, and resets the pad repeat (system_pad_init_repeat). Returns 1 when done, 0 while waiting. Side effects: frees, card view, pad repeat. Callers: 1 (deck_recipe_viewer_update). |
| `0x000008f4` | `void deck_recipe_viewer_scan_imported_decks(void)` | Import step (state 0) on 0x0000c5e2: 0 save_import_update_scan(1, &count 0x0000d908) until done; 1 with decks allocates and clears a 0x1c000-byte buffer 0x0000d904, without decks skips to the end; 2 save_import_update_load(buffer, &count) until done; 3 builds an 8-byte record at 0x0000d90c + 8 x i for each imported deck (0x1c0 bytes each, deck struct at +0x100) with deck_recipe_viewer_count_owned_copies; then clears the step and sets state 1. Returns nothing. Side effects: import buffer, records, state. Callers: 1 (deck_recipe_viewer_update). |
| `0x00000aa4` | `void deck_recipe_viewer_handle_list_input(void)` | Deck-list input (state 3), list of the current tab (0x0000c5ea bits 0-1: 0 stored decks, 1 recipes, 2 imported decks; first visible u16 0x0000c5ec + 4 x tab, cursor row +2, 8 rows). Holding Up/Down accelerates the pad repeat. Cancel sets the cancelled bit, fades out and goes to state 6 (sound 0x7a06). Confirm by mode: mode 1 on an empty slot goes to state 5 (save), on a used slot opens dialog 2; mode 2 opens dialog 1 for a used stored deck, and a dialog (type hidden by Ghidra) for a valid recipe or imported deck; mode 0 on a used stored deck opens the options menu (dialog 5). R or Square / L cycle the tabs when more than one exists (sound 0x7a0d); Right enters the card view (state 4); Up/Down move the cursor and scroll with wrap. Any cursor change reloads the deck view and resets the card cursor and reveal counter. Returns nothing. Side effects: cursor fields, state, dialogs, sounds. Callers: 1 (deck_recipe_viewer_update). |
| `0x000010f8` | `void deck_recipe_viewer_handle_card_input(void)` | Card-view input (state 4) over 7 rows (0-3 main, 4-5 extra, 6 side; row 0x0000c5f9, column 0x0000c5f8): cancel returns to the list (state 3, sound 0x7a06); confirm on a card sets 0x0000c5e5 bit 2 to run the card viewer (sound 0x7a05); Left at column 0 returns to the list, else moves left; Right, Up and Down move inside the row widths (deck_recipe_viewer_get_row_columns). Then updates the text-panel card. Returns nothing. Side effects: cursor, state, sounds. Callers: 1 (deck_recipe_viewer_update). |
| `0x00001314` | `void deck_recipe_viewer_run_card_viewer(void)` | Card viewer steps on 0x0000c5e2: 0 fade out; 1 when done opens the viewer on the cursor card (ui_card_viewer_open with two resources, ui_card_viewer_set_pack_source(resource 0x8015), ui_card_viewer_enable_artwork_select) and sets 0x0000c5e5 bit 3; 2 runs ui_card_viewer_update until done, clears bit 3 and fades in; 3 waits, then clears the step and bit 2. Returns nothing. Side effects: fades, viewer, flags. Callers: 1 (deck_recipe_viewer_update). |
| `0x000014b4` | `void deck_recipe_viewer_save_to_slot(void)` | Save into a stored-deck slot (state 5) on 0x0000c5e2: 0 blinks the cursor for 18 frames (0x0000c5e5 bit 4); 1 calls the save callback 0x0000e128(slot) when set, rebuilds the slot's record (0x0000d772 + 8 x slot) from save_get_stored_deck, reloads the deck view and resets the card cursor and reveal counter; 2 opens dialog 3 and returns to state 3. | `0x00001814` | `void deck_recipe_viewer_load_selected_deck(void)` | Builds the deck view 0x0000c5fe (0x21e bytes) for the selected entry of the current tab: an imported deck (buffer +0x100 of record i), a recipe (deck_load_recipe and deck_get_recipe_card_list) or a used stored deck (struct at +0x54, card list at +0x114), with deck_recipe_viewer_build_deck_view; clears it when nothing is selected. | `0x00001ab0` | `int deck_recipe_viewer_compare_cards(ushort *a, ushort *b)` | qsort comparator for card ids: by card_db_get_frame, equal frames by card_db_get_sort_position. Returns the difference. No side effects. No direct caller (passed to system_qsort). |
| `0x00001b24` | `void deck_recipe_viewer_count_owned_copies(uint *deck, void *record)` | Collects the main, side and extra ids of a 0xc0-byte deck struct, sorts them (deck_recipe_viewer_compare_cards) and counts owned copies against save_get_card_count (or, in owner mode, deck_get_other_deck_card_allowance(owner, id, 0x0000c820)): the first copy of an id counts when any is owned, each further copy k while the owned count differs from k (hypothesis, medium: intended as copies owned). Stores that count at record +5 and the total card count at record +6. Returns nothing. Side effects: record. Callers: 3 (deck_recipe_viewer_start, deck_recipe_viewer_save_to_slot, deck_recipe_viewer_scan_imported_decks). |

| `0x00010210` | `void * deck_select_card_draw_card_row_detailed(void *list, short x, short y, ushort width, ushort *entry)` | Draws one detailed list row (pane 0 in list layout, pane 1 in list layout from layout 4 up): frame plate (ui_draw_card_frame_plate at x + 0x17, resource 0x8006); thumbnail (shop_draw_card_thumb; in mode 0 tinted 0xa0ffffff, with the state as mark, for entries whose byte 5 bits 2-4 are not 4); the name in black at scale 0xc at (x + 0x1d, y + 2), width - 0x36 wide; for frames 7-8 the attribute and property icons, otherwise the attribute and race icons, level badge and ATK/DEF (shop_draw_stat_value). When width is at least 0x110, three count badges (shop_draw_results_row_badges, records copied from the template 0x000195e8): in mode 0 the template badge, the copies in list 1 (deck_count_list_copies(0x0001972c), kind 5) and the owned count (save_get_card_count, kind 6); in modes 1-2 the copies in member 0's deck list 1, or list 3 with kind 3 for fusion and synchro cards, the copies in its list 2 (deck_get_member_slot_count / deck_get_member_slot_card), each coloured 0xff0080ff when the save card list holds the card with state 1 (3 for list 3) or 2, and the copies in list 0 (deck_count_list_copies(0x0001970c)). The thumbnail copy count is shown for narrower rows and, in modes 1-2, when the active pane shows list 1. Then the check icon (shop_draw_check_icon state 2 at x + width - 0x18, y + 3) when save_test_card_byte1_flag is set and the thumbnail badges (shop_draw_card_badges; its is_new argument is save_get_card_state called with the comparison card id == 1, not with the id). Returns the advanced display-list pointer. Side effects: GE and font commands. Callers: 1 (deck_select_card_draw_list_rows_detailed). |

| `0x000109c0` | `void * deck_select_card_draw_list_rows_compact(void *list, void *pane)` | Draws the visible rows of a compact list pane (pane 1 in layouts 2-3): from the first visible index (+6) up to the visible-row count (+0x18) or the end of the list, one row per entry (deck_select_card_draw_card_row_compact at pane x + cell offset, y advancing by the row height +0x1c, width +0x1a). Returns the advanced display-list pointer. Side effects: GE and font commands. Callers: 1 (ui_draw_editor_panes). |
